</style></head><body><article id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-9c36-d730c9d45e46" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Close</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a31c-f47244ebd765" class="">41) Missing deepest layer: <strong>causal-direction is a boundary condition on allowed interventions</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a8e4-e185488d0a1a" class="">Entropy/records is descriptive. The load-bearing object for “direction” is: which transformations are <em>physically and policy-feasibly</em> implementable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a78b-fb558cf481cf" class="">Let the universe be a causal model with state , observations , actions .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-bb9d-c882ce989def" class="">Define two reachable sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-b4ca-cdd367003227" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Forward reachability (intervention-feasible)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-aef5-f18b8a49d5ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}^+(x_t)=\{x_{t+\Delta}:\exists\ A_{t:t+\Delta-1}\ \text{s.t.}\ x_{t+\Delta}=T(x_t,A)+\epsilon\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-a805-c136b23edc62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Backward reachability (retrodiction-only)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-820d-e04d1ca47f54" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}^-(x_t)=\{x_{t-\Delta}:\exists\ \hat{x}_{t-\Delta}=G(O_{\le t})\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8e36-fe22274af146" class="">Arrow-of-time for an agent is the asymmetry:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8dcd-e49830b288f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\mathcal{R}^+(x_t)| \neq |\mathcal{R}^-(x_t)|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-906b-dbb1922aeaa6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-b621-cefb41b2afd0" class="">42) Missing invariants class: <strong>symmetry + gauge invariants (what survives across time/space)</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-ae94-f49ab8ad31d7" class="">If you want invariants that persist across civilizations, biology, and machines, you want invariants that survive coordinate changes and representation shifts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-8c5c-ea06f1314762" class="">Let a representation change be . A true invariant is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-846d-c29b9e6619c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\phi(x))=I(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8059-d631f108d252" class="">Practical invariant families AMOS can compute:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b478-feb8847ed631" class="">(A) Conservation / continuity invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-aa69-e3d469c29467" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla\cdot J + \frac{\partial \rho}{\partial t}=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-a37a-e92fec1ae01e" class="">(B) Gauge-like invariants (same reality, different encodings)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-92f2-ee505fe7679e" class="">If internal state is  and encoding is :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-8157-f7eb3346aab7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s \sim g\cdot s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9645-ed0c030fa67c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(s)=I(g\cdot s)\ \ \forall g</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-8407-e34f838b1031" class="">This closes “different cultures describe the same thing differently” into a formal requirement: compare only gauge-invariant features.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-89e4-cff4c7f0c562"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-81b0-f19e35c18fc3" class="">43) Missing deep measure: <strong>information geometry as the “integrity metric”</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ae4f-e99fefa5f0a3" class="">You want a scalar that tells you whether the system is stable under perturbation, across micro/macro.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-829f-cb7c90d82de2" class="">Let beliefs or model parameters be . The Fisher information metric:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-bdb2-fa2bcd356435" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{G}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_\theta \log p_\theta(O)\ \nabla_\theta \log p_\theta(O)^\top\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8841-fa97e5a655bb" class="">Define “integrity radius” as the size of perturbation that does not collapse predictions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-b88a-d64e22a843e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r(\theta)=\sup\{r:\ D_{KL}(p_{\theta}\|p_{\theta+\delta})\le \epsilon\ \forall \|\delta\|\le r\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a3d8-e22b0dbccf2e" class="">Then integrity is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9352-df509a51c612" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Integrity}(\theta)\propto r(\theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-9481-d9b5875f2617" class="">This is the most compact bridge across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8568-d2d57a942266" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cosmic predictability windows,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a2b9-dd79d81b9a98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biological decoder stability,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-b297-dda6cb51b136" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">civilizational narrative stability,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-aa0e-c17b4ebe23a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">machine model robustness.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9ec8-edab59fbd9ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-b8c9-c4c1b1bbb830" class="">44) Missing irreversible gate: <strong>the write/erase asymmetry</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-96aa-dd35328502fa" class="">“Records” require writing plus maintenance. The overlooked ceiling is: you can write faster than you can reliably erase without destroying structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-ba43-de99d3afbcae" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b8e8-eeda3a0b48df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: write rate (bits/s)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b66c-d03dee8cfe93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: erase/repair rate (bits/s)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-bad4-fe193789ca85" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: corruption rate (bits/s)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-8dd1-f6d9f135d2da" class="">Record stability requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-acbb-ed4fa2b5041c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_t \ge N_t \quad\text{and}\quad W_t \le U_t&#x27; \ (\text{remaining writable DOF per time})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a78d-f562efc3a90f" class="">Most frameworks omit the second inequality:  is bounded by the <em>availability of fresh degrees</em> (physical + social + cognitive).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9213-f2468876c7d2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-bfce-c026068569d5" class="">45) Missing sensory closure (visual/sound/EM): <strong>all sensing is a channel with a decoder</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8b60-f6292e607177" class="">Unify WiFi, vision, sound, “subtle perception,” instruments, and social sensing under one form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8c4f-f3fe8e4ab133" class="">Channel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9cc1-ef2dd965fe07" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t = \mathcal{C}(X_t, E_t) + \nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-929d-debf299aeb92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{X}_t = \mathcal{D}(O_{\le t}; z_t, K_t, \Pi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8ce8-e5c13d766b40" class="">Capacity bound (any modality):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a5ec-d6929122c893" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;O)\le C_{\text{channel}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-82f3-c26aad6387db" class="">Decoder-fidelity bound:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-ab7d-ec3a15b001bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{usable}} \le I(X;O)\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{Fidelity}(z_t)\ge \theta]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{OwnerGate}(K_t,\Pi_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-aa63-eccad741a957" class="">So “not in mainstream science” can be represented as: low consensus instrumentation + low shared decoder calibration, not “nonexistent.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-98f3-ea5503d6f215"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-ae99-d92c65750934" class="">46) Missing cross-scale coupling: <strong>micro → macro via renormalization (coarse-grain stability)</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-9bab-e3e3365c6415" class="">To truly go “across time and space,” you need the rule that maps micro laws to macro loops.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8992-f0e85f9b180d" class="">Let microstate distribution be . Coarse-grain operator  produces macro variables :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8eb4-f809fe8de6cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y=\mathcal{B}(x), \quad p(y)=\sum_{x:\mathcal{B}(x)=y}p(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-850a-e861b2161f32" class="">A macro-loop is valid only if it is stable under refinement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-9303-fa6f8178c1a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F_{\text{macro}}(\mathcal{B}(x)) \approx \mathcal{B}(F_{\text{micro}}(x))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-84ee-d20bf49ae401"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8ae4-d6ec8b6c99c2" class="">47) Missing self/non-self boundary: <strong>self is a control boundary, not a substance</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a1f8-df1f2376f373" class="">Define boundary  as control separability between “inside” and “outside.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9216-fc83d09a4a1f" class="">Inside dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-8cf0-f35f5bcbea51" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{in}_{t+1}=F^{in}(x^{in}_t, a_t) + \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-936c-e0c78cce7b43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{out}_{t+1}=F^{out}(x^{out}_t) + \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-af14-d86621e3ca67" class="">Boundary is strong when the agent can reduce prediction error about  using actions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-855e-ff239035d559" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t \propto \Delta \big(\text{Err}(x^{in})\big)\ \text{under interventions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-9bf5-df762965ccaa" class="">Non-self is where actions do not reliably reduce error. This closes “self and non-self” without metaphysics while still allowing “intangible” as boundary-weak but information-present.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9b7e-d067655f060c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-bc51-c496670501df" class="">48) Missing cross-species law: <strong>alignment is shared-code formation</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8ab6-ec95562943d8" class="">Cross-species “inheritance” becomes: do two organisms converge to compatible decoders for a shared environment?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-8cb3-df91e47d27ce" class="">Let decoders be . Shared-code score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-90ca-d07a72877d9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{SC}(t)=I(\hat{X}^h_t;\hat{X}^a_t\mid O_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-8463-df80ba3ba77f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}\text{SC}(t)\ge 0\ \text{over a window}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-9ac5-e4ee3e3d380c" class="">This is measurable in principle (behavioral synchrony, prediction agreement) without requiring claims beyond evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-aa85-e5b277c95b5b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a548-edabd9f29661" class="">49) Missing cosmic/earth layer: <strong>planet is a constraint-and-channel environment</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a8c4-d0466b5d56e5" class="">Instead of “Earth as context,” treat it as a structured constraint generator :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a2d8-d663fe62bff2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t+1}=F(X_t) + H(X_t;\ \mathcal{E}_\oplus(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8910-f94062f2f543" class="">Where  includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a8e2-e16d104c72fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gravity constraints (mechanical budgets),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-aff0-d870abc9e17c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM environment (noise floors + channels),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-89d1-ee2bc21f4f97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biosphere coupling (co-regulation networks),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-81c4-c9721f1fadf7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">institutional layers (policy + memory systems).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-aee2-d2af1910bad1" class="">This is how you connect cosmology → planet → organism → civilization in one equation family.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b5c9-d9df8678defb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-a805-c8840c97398f" class="">50) The “Ultra” unified matrix (now with the missing blocks)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-95df-e340ab74bcc4" class="">Your previous matrix becomes a block system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg=="
